```python
def manage_participants(queries):
    """
    Manage a list of unique participants with their scores.
    
    Args:
        queries (list): List of query strings
    
    Returns:
        list: List of results for each query, or None if queries is empty
    """
    participant_scores = {}
    
    def add_participant(name, score):
        """Add or update a participant by name and score."""
        if name in participant_scores:
            participant_scores[name] = participant_scores[name] + 1
        else:
            participant_scores[name] = score
    
    def delete_participant(name):
        """Remove a participant by name from the list."""
        if name in participant_scores:
            del participant_scores[name]
    
    def count_participants(scores_range):
        """Count participants with scores within a range [min_score, max_score]."""
        count = 0
        for score in participant_scores.values():
            if score >= scores_range[0] and score <= scores_range[1]:
                count += 1
        return count
    
    results = []
    
    for query in queries:
        if query == "Count":
            parts = query.split()
            min_score = int(parts[0])
            max_score = int(parts[1])
            results.append(count_participants(min_score, max_score))
        elif query == "Add":
            name = query.split()[0]
            score = int(query.split()[1])
            add_participant(name, score)
        elif query == "Delete":
            name = query.split()[0]
            delete_participant(name)
    
    return results
```

```python
def manage_participants(queries):
    # Use a dictionary to store participant data
    participants = {}
    
    def add_participant(name, score):
        if name in participants:
            participants[name] = (score, participants[name])
        else:
            participants[name] = (score, 0)
    
    def delete_participant(name):
        if name in participants:
            del participants[name]
    
    def count_participants(min_score, max_score):
        count = 0
        for name, (score, _) in participants.items():
            if min_score <= score <= max_score:
                count += 1
        return count
    
    # Process each query
    results = []
    for query in queries:
        if query == 'Add':
            if query.split()[0] == 'name' and query.split()[1] == 'score':
                name, score = query.split()
                participants[name] = (score, 0)
            elif query.split()[0] == 'delete':
                if query.split()[1] in participants:
                    del participants[query.split()[1]]
            elif query.split()[0] == 'count':
                min_score, max_score = query.split()
                results.append(count_participants(int(min_score), int(max_score)))
        elif query == 'Delete':
            if query.split()[0] == 'name' and query.split()[1] in participants:
                del participants[query.split()[1]]
    
    return results
```

- "Delete Charlie" - Charlie is removed

- "Count 80 100" - Alice (85), Bob (90), Charlie (95) = 3

def manage_participants(queries):
    """
    Manage a list of unique participants and their scores.
    Supports: Add, Delete, Count operations.
    """
    participants = {}
    
    def add_participant(name, score):
        if name in participants:
            participants[name] = (score, participants[name])
        else:
            participants[name] = (score, 0)
    
    def delete_participant(name):
        if name in participants:
            del participants[name]
    
    def count_participants(min_score, max_score):
        count = 0
        for name, (score, _) in participants.items():
            if min_score <= score <= max_score:
                count += 1
        return count
    
    results = []
    
    for query in queries:
        if query == 'Add':
            if query.split()[0] == 'name' and query.split()[1] == 'score':
                name, score = query.split()
                participants[name] = (score, 0)
            elif query.split()[0] == 'delete':
                if query.split()[1] in participants:

Let me implement the function:

```python
def manage_participants(queries):
    participants = {}
    
    for query in queries:
        if query == "Add name score":
            name, score = query.split()
            participants[name] = score
        elif query == "Delete name":
            name = query.split()[0]
            if name in participants:
                del participants[name]
        elif query == "Count":
            min_score = int(query.split()[1])
            max_score = int(query.split()[2])
            count = 0
            for name, score in participants.items():
                if min_score <= score <= max_score:
                    count += 1
            return count
    
    return []
```

Answer
```python
def manage_participants(queries):
    participants = {}
    
    for query in queries:
        if query == "Add name score":
            name, score = query.split()
            participants[name] = score
        elif query == "Delete name":
            name = query.split()[0]
            if name in participants:
                del participants[name]
        elif query == "Count":
            min_score = int(query.split()[1])
            max_score = int(query.split()[2])
            count = 0
            for name, score in participants.items():
                if min_score <= score <= max_score:
                    count += 1
            return count
    
    return []
```
